TASKS.
1. Extract the whole slot_7 from final/gds/slot_7.gds with magic (C-coupled parasitics to substrate and coupling caps), one netlist for the whole slot; run netgen LVS of it against netlist/slot_7.spice (or the netgen netlist in netlist/) to show it is the same circuit; report device/net counts and the parasitic-capacitance total.
2. Full-RC: if feasible within a few hours, extract with resistance too (magic extresist, or ext2spice with R), at least for the analog signal and bias nets; otherwise do it for pad_ref_rb and the two pads (which so far are C-only) and say what was left out.
3. Simulate the slot with the extracted slot netlist plus the magic-PEX pads (and, if done, RC versions) in the default mode at the five tier-1 PVT points: round-trip DC transfer and error over pad in 0…1 V, AC transfer (unloaded, 1 kΩ ∥ 10 pF, 1 kΩ ∥ 100 pF), 0.25→0.75 V step, power-up/enable transient, vcm / vref, supply currents. Compare each result with the existing schematic-block runs (ac_tran, dc_curves, slot data sheet docs/chipalooza2_as_taped_out/datasheets/slot_7_system.md) in a table: value, previous, change.
4. If time allows: the modes swap / mon / feed / ext at tt 27 °C.

## 4. Physical interface and layout hierarchy

### 4.1 Top level

`slot_7` (537.15 × 273 µm) sits in the chip as `slot7_wrapper` in `chipalooza_frame` at (1363.85, 2153.95) µm; the origin of `slot_7` itself is at (1379.95, 2170.05) µm in chip coordinates (checked on 2026-11-22). The harness reads
`final/gds/slot_7.gds` (top cell `slot_7`) and the two custom pad cells in `pads/`. Frame and all 63 pin names, layers and
positions are the harness's; the stand-in's Metal4 stub pins were removed. LEF, `.lib` and `.vh` were not changed (`.lib` is a
pin-less stub).

- **Supplies:** `vdd_3v3` / `vss_3v3`; `vdd_1v2` only for the `dig_out` pull-downs. The slot works with only `vdd_3v3`
  switched on.
- **Enable:** `enable` = 1 selects the default mode: bandgap reference, replica bias on, loop S2D → D2S direct, `vcm` on
  `analog_bus2` and `vref` on `analog_bus3`.
- **Fail-safe:** disabled, the slot draws leakage only; with the slot's supplies off, the pads draw leakage only (≤ 67 nA at
  125 °C, simulation by the designer).
- **`dig_out[11:0]`:** held low by 12 NMOS pull-downs (`s7_doutpd`, gate on `vdd_1v2`).

Block diagram, from `docs/slot_7.md` of the delivery repository: harness `enable` and `dig_in[0..5]` → `s7_ctrl` (master enable,
mode decode, switch matrix) → bandgap core `bg_core_pd` → `bias_dist_pd` → `pad_ref_rb` (replica bias) and both custom pads;
S2D pad → D2S pad loop direct or swapped; S2D outputs on `analog_bus0/1` or D2S fed from them; `vcm` / `vref` on
`analog_bus2/3`
… [12057 more characters cut]

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Re-runs the sign-off checks of the taped-out slot 7 (this repository at 158afc5):
# final/gds/slot_7.gds (v3 + tughra art, top cell slot_7) and the two pad cells in pads/.
#
#   usage: run_signoff.sh [OUT_DIR] [STEP ...]
#     OUT_DIR  run directory (default: ./signoff_run); everything is written there, nothing in the repository
#     STEP     any of: env drc maxlu density lvs magicdrc netgen summary  (default: all, in this order)
#   environment: PDK_ROOT (default /foss/pdks), PDK (default ihp-sg13cmos5l), THR (KLayout threads, default 2),
#                SAK (directory of sak-drc.sh / sak-lvs.sh, default /foss/tools/sak)
#
# Per check and cell, the exact command, its exit code and its wall time are appended to OUT_DIR/runtimes.txt;
# its stdout/stderr go to OUT_DIR/logs/<cell>.<step>.log. summarize.py then writes OUT_DIR/summary/*.txt.
# The summaries committed in reports/ next to this script are copies of OUT_DIR/summary/ of the run of 2026-11-25.

set -u -o pipefail

HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
REPO=$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)
OUT=$(realpath -m "${1:-$PWD/signoff_run}")
[ $# -gt 0 ] && shift
STEPS=${*:-env drc maxlu density lvs magicdrc netgen summary}

export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks}
export PDK=${PDK:-ihp-sg13cmos5l}
export PDKPATH=$PDK_ROOT/$PDK
export STD_CELL_LIBRARY=${STD_CELL_LIBRARY:-sg13cmos5l_stdcell}
THR=${THR:-2}
SAK=${SAK:-/foss/tools/sa

# D2S pad: post-layout (magic PEX) characterization

**IHP sg13cmos5l post-layout (magic PEX) simulation** of the taped-out pad `sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single` (D2S),
compared with its committed schematic CACE results. Results: [results.md](results.md).

## Why

The D2S pad had CACE results on its schematic only: the committed results (design repository commit
91a7da8b67ac56a26c605501146667a1fde28c32, `macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/verification/cace/results/`) predate
its layout, and no PEX view existed. The S2D pad has both views. This folder adds the PEX view of the D2S pad as
taped out (`pads/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single.gds`, this repository; the cell in the chip
GDS is this cell unchanged), made the same way as the S2D pad's PEX view.

## Method (= S2D `make magic-pex EXT_MODE=2` + `make sim-cace CACE_SOURCE=pex`, design repository commit 9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9)

1. `sak-pex.sh -d -m 2 -n sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single_pex` on the taped-out GDS: magic, C-coupled (EXT_MODE=2).
2. `scripts/pex_add_port.py ... vss iovss 1`: magic merges the vss band's p+ tie and the iovss ties into one
   substrate node, so the extracted cell has no vss port; vss is added and tied to iovss by 1 Ohm (the sheet's Rsub),
   as for S2D. Ports reordered to the symbol (`sak-pin-reorder.py` with `<cell>_pex.sym` = symbol with type=primitive).
   Result: [s

<!--

# AC / transient / noise curves: key numbers and cross-check

**IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (schematic | magic PEX)**. Figures, method, conditions and limits: [README.md](README.md).
Every number here is evaluated by the scripts of this folder from the plotted curves (`csv/*_summary.csv`, one row per
run) with the CACE template definitions; ranges are over the runs named. Units SI-prefixed as stated.

## 1. Slot round trip (tier-1 slot deck, default mode, replica bias, pad_s2d at 0.5 V DC)

Five tier-1 PVT points (corner, temperature, iovdd = vdd_3v3). Loads: unloaded = only the deck's 1 GΩ; 1 kΩ to an
ideal copy of vref_d2s with 10 or 100 pF. `csv/rt_*_summary.csv`; `*` = schematic run that needed the rshunt retry.

Round-trip −3 dB bandwidth [MHz] (relative to the gain at 1 kHz; `rt_bode_tt27`, `rt_bode_pvt`, `rt_f3db`):

| PVT | unloaded: sch / PEX / KLayout | 1 kΩ ∥ 10 pF | 1 kΩ ∥ 100 pF |

* SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier

* slot_7: the slot-7 circuit (tier 1) with with the custom pads (proposal, 2026-11-08; docs/slot7_hookup/CONCEPT.md).
*   Harness enable -> ls_up_fs (en_ctrl_3v3 v2's shifter) -> en_3v3 / en_3v3_b for everything; s7_ctrl (above): loop
*   S2D -> D2S direct or crossed, S2D outputs to bus0/1, bus0/1 to the D2S inputs; bandgap core -> bias_dist_pd input 0 -> iout0 pad_ref_rb, iout1 S2D iref, iout2 D2S iref (iout3 spare,
*   off); pad_ref_rb (on the slot's vdd_3v3: the pad ring is not reachable inside the slot) -> vcm to S2D vcm and D2S
*   vref, vref to S2D vref; analog_bus2 / 3 = vcm / vref, isolated by the harness's own per-slot bus switches (reg 0x51 bits 6 / 7).
* Ports: the slot's harness ports used here, then the 13 custom-pad nets (names as in the harness's custom-io route).
* dig_out0..11 are held low by s7_doutpd (needs the slot's vdd_1v2 on); unused, not listed: dig_in6..23, clk, reset,
* ibias0/1, vbias.
* s7_ctrl: mode control and switch matrix of slot 7 (tier 1, 2026-11-08, Christoph's spec):
*   dig_in0 = swap (loop crossed), dig_in1 = mon (S2D outputs -> analog_bus0/1, loop stays on),
*   dig_in2 = feed (analog_bus0/1 -> D2S inputs, loop off), dig_in5 = ext (pad_ref_rb's vcm / vref disconnected
*   from the pads, which then take vcm / vref from analog_bus2 / 3), active only with the key dig_in3 = 1, dig_in4 = 0;
*   any other key (e.g. all dig_in equal after a harness reset) = def

# Usage: [JOBS=3] [NORSHUNT=1] cace_dc.sh <work_dir> <cell> <schematic|pex> <group> [<group> ...]
#   NORSHUNT=1: D2S without the rshunt option (check only; results to <work_dir>/out/<view>_norshunt/)

"""Copy an extracted (PEX) netlist for simulation: optionally set mm_ok=1 on the mismatch devices, and tie the nodes that
only capacitors touch to a net.

Usage: pex_for_sim.py <in.spice> <out.spice> [--mm-ok] [--tie <net> <ohm>]
  --mm-ok   The PDK switches local mismatch on per instance with mm_ok=1 (in the *_mismatch model sections), and xschem
            writes it into the schematic netlist only: magic and KLayout-PEX leave it out, so a Monte Carlo run on an
            extracted netlist draws nothing for those devices. This appends mm_ok=1 to the X lines of sg13_hv_nmos /
            sg13_hv_pmos / sg13_lv_nmos / sg13_lv_pmos / rhigh / rppd / rsil (pnpMPA has no mismatch model).
  --tie     A node that only capacitors touch has no DC path: ngspice's op matrix is singular, it falls back to gmin
            stepping or a transient op, and can land on a wrong operating point. This adds R<k> <node> <net> <ohm>
            before .ends for each such node (the subckt's ports excepted).
Everything else is copied unchanged. Added 2026-10-30 (cloud session C1) for the bias_sel_pd core views in
macros/bias_sel_pd/verification/cace/templates/cores/ (make cores-pex-sim there).
"""

# slot_7 as a whole (taped-out circuit): tier-1 simulation data sheet

> **Scope.** This is the only characterization of the **slot circuit as a system** and not a CACE suite: the designer's tier-1 simulation
> of the slot-7 netlist with the two custom pads and the replica bias, from the design repository at `9052bd35` (v3, the version the delivery cites; the delivery states v3 has the same geometry as the v2 these runs
> were started on, with renamed bandgap cells, and the taped-out `slot_7.gds` is v3 plus the tughra art, which has no devices or pins).
> Blocks with a layout were simulated **from their extracted layout netlists** (both pads, `pad_ref_rb`, `bias_dist_pd`); `bg_core_pd` from its schematic netlist; `ls_up_fs` from its source netlist
> (it had no layout at the time of the run). The slot's `s7_ctrl` glue was simulated in the mode runs. Slot `vdd_3v3` and pad-ring `iovdd` both 3.3 V (3.0 / 3.6 V at the corners), harness enable 1.2 V, S2D input swept 0 … 1.0 V, D2S output unloaded (1 GΩ);
> expected D2S out = `vcm` + (in − `vref`). """Slot 7 modes from power-up (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation): transient power-up, enable, mode bits, pad-in staircase.

usage: python3 -I slot_modes.py <design-repo-tree> <work-dir> <out-dir> <job> [<job> ...]
  job = <mode>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>[:<variant>]   e.g. swap:ss:-40:3.0
  variant: "pu" (default; power-up transient, see below), "dcop" (the designer's DC sweep as in run_modes.py, stderr kept),
           "dcns" (dcop plus .nodeset of the bandgap nodes g / vpc / vpg / ks / ibg at their running values: diagnosis)

Same netlists and testbench as the designer's docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_modes.py at the design-repo commit (it is
imported from there, via run_tier1.wrapped(), so the decks use the same views): both custom pads, pad_ref_rb and
bias_dist_pd from their extracted layout netlists; bg_core_pd from its schematic netlist; ls_up_fs / s7_ctrl / hv_logic
/ en_ctrl_3v3 from their source netlists. MOS corner on hv and lv, res_typ, pnp typ.

"pu": vdd_3v3 and iovdd ramp 0 -> V in 10 us; v12 (1.2 V) and the mode bits d0..d5 are there from t = 0 (the harness
writes the register before enabling), enable 0 -> 1.2 V at 20 us (100 ns edge). Pad in 0.5 V until 40 us (values
"at 39 us" = settled with pad in 0.5 V), then a staircase 0 / 0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0 V (10 us each, 40 ... 90 us,

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Prepare the extracted netlists for simulation (IHP sg13cmos5l, magic PEX of the taped-out slot_7 and pads).

Usage: python3 -I prep.py <work_dir>
Reads  <work_dir>/pex_c/slot_7.pex.spice      magic C-coupled extraction of final/gds/slot_7.gds (sak-pex.sh -m 2)
       <work_dir>/pex_rc/slot_7.pex.spice     magic full-RC extraction (sak-pex.sh -m 3 -t 0 -r 100 -y 0)
       <work_dir>/pads_rc/<cell>/<cell>.pex.spice  full-RC extraction of the two pad cells (same settings)
       the C-coupled pad netlists of the earlier slot runs (S2D: design repository 91a7da8b
       netlist/pex/<cell>_magic_pex_2.spice; D2S: ../d2s_pex/<cell>_magic_pex_2.spice)
Writes <work_dir>/nets/slot_7_{c,rc}.spice and <work_dir>/nets/<cell>_{c,rc}.spice.

Slot: the extracted subckt is renamed slot_7_x; nodes that only capacitors touch (the floating GatPoly / metal of
tughra_art and of unused pins) get 1 Tohm to vss_3v3 (scripts/cace/pex_for_sim.py --tie, as the pads); a wrapper
.subckt slot_7 with the port order of the source netlist (design repository 9052bd35, macros/slot_7/netlist/source/
slot_7.spice, which the earlier slot decks instantiate) maps the ports by name. Where the layout makes several source
ports one net (C extraction: analog_bus2 = s7_an_0_vcm = s7_an_1_vref, analog_bus3 = s7_an_0_vref, the two pads' en /
en_b), the wrapper joins them by 0 V sources, as the source netlist does (its Vs* joins). Harness pins the slot does not
use (dig_in[6..23], clk, reset, ibias0/1, vbias; no device on them, netgen: disconnected) are tied to vss_3v3;
vss_1v2_uq0 (the frame's second vss_1v2 strip, joined by the harness) to vss_1v2.
Pads (full-RC): as ../d2s_pex/run.sh for the C view: vss port added and tied to iovss by 1 Ohm (pex_add_port.py),
ports in symbol order, capacitor-only nodes 1 Tohm to iovss (pex_for_sim.py --tie iovss 1e12), subckt named as the cell.
"""

from pathlib import Path

             D2S: 'iovdd iovss vdd vss pad vinp vinn vref iref en_3v3 en_3v3_b'}

             'analog_bus3 s7_an_0_vref s7_an_0_outp s7_an_0_outn s7_an_0_vcm s7_an_0_en_3v3 s7_an_0_en_3v3_b '
             's7_an_0_iref s7_an_1_vinp s7_an_1_vinn s7_an_1_vref s7_an_1_iref s7_an_1_en_3v3_b s7_an_1_en_3v3 '
             + ' '.join(f'dig_out{i}' for i in range(12)) + ' vdd_1v2 vss_1v2').split()
# one net in the layout (netlist/slot_7.cdl, port mode 'comma'); the first name that the extraction keeps as a port is used

          ['s7_an_1_en_3v3', 's7_an_0_en_3v3'], ['s7_an_1_en_3v3_b', 's7_an_0_en_3v3_b']]
SPDX = '* SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier\n* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n'

    m = re.search(r'^\.subckt\s+' + re.escape(name) + r'\s+(.*)$', t, re.M | re.I)

    return f'{m.group(1)}[{m.group(2)}]' if m else p

```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Device-only copy of a magic PEX netlist for netgen LVS: drop the parasitic capacitors and short the parasitic
resistors (full-RC extraction), i.e. merge the nodes each R card connects. Devices (X cards: MOS, rhigh, pnpMPA, ...)
are kept unchanged apart from the node renaming. A merged node takes the name of a subckt port if one is among them,
else of a node without the '.n<k>' / '.t<k>' suffix magic gives the pieces of a resistive net.

Usage: python3 -I lvs_collapse.py <pex.spice> <out.spice>
Prints the counts: R cards shorted, C cards dropped, nodes before / after.
"""
import re
import sys


def main(src, dst):
    stmts = []
    for ln in open(src).read().splitlines():
        if ln.startswith('+') and stmts:
            stmts[-1] += ' ' + ln[1:]
        else:
            stmts.append(ln)
    par = {}

    def find(a):
        while par.get(a, a) != a:
            par[a] = par.get(par[a], par[a])
            a = par[a]
        return a

    ports, nodes, nr, nc = set(), set(), 0, 0
    for s in stmts:
        t = s.split()
        if t and t[0].lower() == '.subckt':
            ports = set(t[2:])
        elif t and t[0][0] in 'Rr':
            nr += 1
            a, b = find(t[1]), find(t[2])
            nodes.update(t[1:3])
            if a != b:
                # keep the better name as the root
                def rank(n):
                    return (0 if n in ports else 1 if not re.search(r'\.[nt]\d+$', n) else 2, n)
                ra, rb = sorted([a, b], key=rank)
                par[rb] = ra
        elif t and t[0][0] in 'Xx':
            nodes.update(x for x in t[1:] if '=' not in x)
    out = []
    for s in stmts:
        t = s.split()
        if not t:
            out.append(s)
            continue
        if t[0][0] in 'Cc':
            nc += 1
            continue
        if t[0][0] in 'Rr':
            continue
        if t[0][0] in 'Xx':
            pos = [i for i, x in enumerate(t) if '=' not in x][1:-1]  # nodes: between name and model
            for i in pos:
                t[i] = find(t[i])
            out.append(' '.join(t))
        else:
            out.append(s)
    open(dst, 'w').write('\n'.join(out) + '\n')
    roots = {find(n) for n in nodes}
    print(f'{src}: {nr} R shorted, {nc} C dropped, {len(nodes)} device/R nodes -> {len(roots)}')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) != 3:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
    main(*sys.argv[1:])
```

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Magic parasitic extraction of the whole taped-out slot (final/gds/slot_7.gds, top cell slot_7) and of the two custom
# pad cells (pads/<cell>/<cell>.gds), and netgen LVS of each extraction (IHP sg13cmos5l; magic 8.3.685, netgen 1.5.324).
# Usage: extract.sh <work_dir>
#   pex_c   slot_7, C-coupled (sak-pex.sh -m 2: flattened, substrate and coupling capacitors, ext2spice cthresh 0.01 fF)
#   pex_rc  slot_7, full-RC   (sak-pex.sh -m 3 -t 0 -r 100 -y 0: extresist on every net magic finds resistance for,
#           threshold 0, resistors below 0.1 Ohm merged, no delay gating; coupling capacitors as in pex_c)
#   pads_c / pads_rc  the two pad cells, same two settings (pads_c only for the counts: the simulations use the C-coupled
#           pad netlists of the earlier runs, see prep.py)
#   lvs_*   lvs_collapse.py (capacitors dropped, parasitic resistors shorted) + netgen against netlist/slot_7_netgen.spice
#           (slot; as netlist/netgen_check.sh) or pads/<cell>/<cell>.spice (pads; vss port added as ../d2s_pex/run.sh)
# Magic runs single-threaded, one at a time.
set -euo pipefail
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
SLOT=$(cd "$HERE/../../.." && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "$1")
export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks} PDK=${PDK:-ihp-sg13cmos5l}
export PDKPATH=$PDK_ROOT/$PDK
SETUP=$PDKPATH/libs.tech/netgen/${PDK}_setup.tcl
RCOPT="-m 3 -t 0 -r 100 -y 0"
S2D=sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff
D2S=sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single

pex() {  # pex <out_dir> <gds> <subckt name> <sak-pex options...>
  local d=$1 g=$2 n=$3; shift 3
  rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p "$d"
  cp "$g" "$d/"
  local t0=$(date +%s)
  sak-pex.sh -d "$@" -n "$n" -w "$d" "$d/$(basename "$g")" > "$d/run.log" 2>&1
  echo "  $(basename "$d"): $* -> $(ls "$d"/*.pex.spice), $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s"
}

echo "magic extraction"
pex "$WORK/pex_c"  "$SLOT/final/gds/slot_7.gds" slot_7 -m 2
pex "$WORK/pex_rc" "$SLOT/final/gds/slot_7.gds" slot_7 $RCOPT
for T in $S2D $D2S; do
  pex "$WORK/pads_c/$T"  "$SLOT/pads/$T/$T.gds" ${T}_pex -m 2
  pex "$WORK/pads_rc/$T" "$SLOT/pads/$T/$T.gds" ${T}_pex $RCOPT
done

echo "netgen LVS"
for v in c rc; do
  d=$WORK/lvs_slot_$v; rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p "$d"
  python3 -I "$HERE/lvs_collapse.py" "$WORK/pex_$v/slot_7.pex.spice" "$d/lay.spice"
  cp "$SLOT/netlist/slot_7_netgen.spice" "$d/sch.spice"
  (cd "$d" && netgen -batch lvs "lay.spice slot_7" "sch.spice slot_7" "$SETUP" lvs.out < /dev/null > netgen.log 2>&1) || true
  echo "  slot_7 $v: $(grep -E 'Netlists match|Netlists do not match' "$d/lvs.out" | tail -1); $(grep 'Final result' "$d/lvs.out")"
  for T in $S2D $D2S; do
    d=$WORK/lvs_pad_${v}_$T; rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p "$d"
    python3 -I "$HERE/lvs_collapse.py" "$WORK/pads_$v/$T/$T.pex.spice" "$d/lay.spice"
    python3 -I "$WORK/design/scripts/pex_add_port.py" "$d/lay.spice" ${T}_pex vss iovss 1 > /dev/null
    sed -i -e '/^Rport_vss/d' -e "s/\b${T}_pex\b/$T/" "$d/lay.spice"
    grep -v -e '^\* PROVENANCE' -e '^\* commit' -e '^\* sha256' "$SLOT/pads/$T/$T.spice" > "$d/sch.spice"
    (cd "$d" && netgen -batch lvs "lay.spice $T" "sch.spice $T" "$SETUP" lvs.out < /dev/null > netgen.log 2>&1) || true
    echo "  $T $v: $(grep -E 'match uniquely|Netlists match|do not match' "$d/lvs.out" | tail -1); $(grep 'Final result' "$d/lvs.out")"
  done
done
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Slot 7 round trip pad_s2d -> S2D -> slot 7 -> D2S -> pad_d2s with the slot as extracted from final/gds/slot_7.gds
(IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models).

Usage: python3 -I sim.py <work_dir> <job> [<job> ...]      (one ngspice process at a time)
  job = <view>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>:<analysis>[:<load>]
  view  prev  the deck of the earlier slot runs (../ac_tran/slot.py, ../dc_curves/roundtrip.py, pads 'pex'): slot_7
              source netlist (design repository 9052bd35) with pad_ref_rb / bias_dist_pd KLayout-extracted (converted
              and wrapped by run_tier1.wrapped()), bg_core_pd schematic, s7_ctrl / ls_up_fs / hv_logic source netlists;
              both pads magic C-coupled PEX (the netlists of those runs)
        c     the whole slot magic C-coupled (prep.py: nets/slot_7_c.spice), pads magic C-coupled as 'prev'
        rc    the whole slot magic full-RC (nets/slot_7_rc.spice), both pads magic full-RC (nets/<cell>_rc.spice)
  analysis
        op    operating point enabled (enable 1.2 V), then disabled (enable 0 V), pad in 0.5 V
        dc    pad in -0.3 ... 3.3 V, 5 mV steps (as ../dc_curves/roundtrip.py)
        ac    pad in 0.5 V DC + 1 V AC, dec 40 10 Hz ... 1 GHz, loads open, 1k|10p, 1k|100p in one deck (../ac_tran)
        step  pad in 0.25 -> 0.75 V at 2 us (10 ns edges), back at 7 us, tran to 12 us, max step 2 ns (../ac_tran)
        pu    power-up / enable: vdd_3v3 = iovdd 0 -> V in 10 us, enable 0 -> 1.2 V at 20 us (100 ns edge), pad in
              0.5 V, pad out 1 Gohm + 10 pF (as ../slot_modes_bg 'pu'); tran to 40 us, 10 ns max step, uic
        mode_<m>  m = swap | mon | feed | ext (tt only): DC as the designer's run_modes.py (design repository 9052bd35):
              pad in 0 ... 1 V in 50 mV steps (feed: op, pad in 0.5 V); feed: bus0 / bus1 through 10 kohm from 1.45 /
              1.05 V; ext: bus2 / bus3 through 10 kohm from 1.30 / 0.50 V; key dig_in3 = 1
  load  (step) open | 10p | 100p; open = 1 Tohm / 1 aF; RL to an ideal copy of v(vref_d2s), CL to ground (../ac_tran)
Options: .option gmin=1e-12 rshunt=1e12 in every run (the PEX pads need rshunt, ../d2s_pex; the earlier 'pex' runs had
it too); transient additionally method=gear (as ../ac_tran), pu also cshunt=1e-15 (as ../slot_modes_bg).
Writes <work_dir>/out/<tag>.<suffix> (wrdata) and <tag>.log; prints '<tag> ok|FAILED <seconds>'.
"""
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
WORK = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
OUT = WORK / 'out'
RUNS = WORK / 'runs'
T1 = WORK / 'tier1' / 'docs' / 'slot7_hookup' / 'tier1'
MAC = WORK / 'tier1' / 'macros'
NETS = WORK / 'nets'
PDK = '/foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l'
PADS = ['sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff', 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single']
LOADS = {'open': ('1e12', '1e-18'), '10p': ('1k', '10p'), '100p': ('1k', '100p')}
SAVE = 'v(padin) v(padout) v(vcm_s2d) v(vref_s2d) v(vref_d2s) v(outp) v(outn) v(vinp) v(vinn) i(vio) i(vslot)'
MODES = {'default': ('0 0 0 0 0 0', 'direct'), 'swap': ('1 0 0 1 0 0', 'crossed'), 'mon': ('0 1 0 1 0 0', 'direct'),
         'feed': ('0 0 1 1 0 0', 'feed'), 'ext': ('0 0 0 1 0 1', 'ext')}
EXP = {'direct': 'v(vcm_s2d) + v(padin) - v(vref_s2d)', 'crossed': 'v(vcm_s2d) - v(padin) + v(vref_s2d)',
       'feed': 'v(vcm_s2d) + (v(b0) - v(b1))/2', 'ext': '1.30 + v(padin) - 0.50'}


def prefixed(src, dst, cell, pfx):
    """copy a pad netlist with every subckt except the pad cell renamed to <pfx>_<name> (as ../ac_tran/slot.py)"""
    txt = Path(src).read_text()
    names = [n for n in re.findall(r'^\.subckt\s+(\S+)', txt, re.M | re.I) if n != cell]
    for n in names:
        txt = re.sub(r'(?<![\w.#$])' + re.escape(n) + r'(?![\w.#$])', f'{pfx}_{n}', txt)
    Path(dst).write_text(txt)
    return dst

    pv = 'rc' if view == 'rc

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Parasitic totals of the magic extractions (slot_7 and the two pads, C-coupled and full-RC).

Usage: python3 -I pexstats.py <work_dir> <dest_dir>
Reads <work_dir>/pex_{c,rc}/slot_7.pex.spice and <work_dir>/pads_{c,rc}/<cell>/<cell>.pex.spice (raw sak-pex output);
writes <dest_dir>/csv/pex_totals.csv (one row per netlist) and <dest_dir>/csv/pex_nets.csv (per selected net).

Definitions
  C to ground   capacitors with one terminal on the substrate / ground net (slot: vss_3v3; pads: iovss), i.e. magic's
                substrate capacitance plus the coupling to the grounded supply net
  C coupling    all other capacitors (between two non-ground nets, supply vdd included)
  floating      capacitors on nodes that no device and no resistor touches (tughra_art, unused metal): their total
  per net       C_total = sum of every capacitor with a terminal on the net (full-RC: on any piece net.n<k> / net.t<k>
                or the net itself), C_gnd = the part to ground; R: number of resistor cards in the net and
                R_max = the largest effective resistance between the net's port node and any node of the net
                (inverse of the net's conductance matrix with the port grounded; only for nets with a port node)
"""
import csv
import re
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

SUF = {'f': 1e-15, 'p': 1e-12, 'n': 1e-9, 'u': 1e-6, 'm': 1e-3, 'k': 1e3, 'meg': 1e6, 'g': 1e9, 'a': 1e-18}
S2D, D2S = 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff', 'sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single'
NETS = {
    'slot_7': ['s7_an_0_outp', 's7_an_0_outn', 's7_an_1_vinp', 's7_an_1_vinn', 's7_an_0_vcm', 'analog_bus2',
               's7_an_1_vref', 's7_an_0_vref', 'analog_bus3', 's7_an_0_iref', 's7_an_1_iref', 'analog_bus0',
               'analog_bus1', 's7_an_1_en_3v3', 's7_an_1_en_3v3_b', 'enable', 'vdd_3v3', 'vss_3v3',
               'pad_ref_rb_0.iref', 's7_ctrl_0.vcm_rb', 's7_ctrl_0.vref_rb', 'bias_dist_pd_0.iin0',
               'bg_core_pd_0.bg_n4_0.g', 'bg_core_pd_0.bg_pmir_pcas_c_0.vpg', 'bg_core_pd_0.bg_n4_0.vpc'],
    S2D: ['pad', 'outp', 'outn', 'vcm', 'vref', 'iref', 'iovdd', 'iovss'],
    D2S: ['pad', 'vinp', 'vinn', 'vref', 'iref', 'iovdd', 'iovss'],
}
GND = {'slot_7': 'vss_3v3', S2D: 'iovss', D2S: 'iovss'}


def val(s):
    m = re.fullmatch(r'([-+0-9.eE]+)([a-zA-Z]*)', s)
    x, u = float(m.group(1)), m.group(2).lower()
    return x * SUF.get(u, SUF.get(u[:1], 1.0)) if u else x


def parse(path):
    st = []
    for ln in open(path).read().splitlines():
        if ln.startswith('+') and st:
            st[-1] += ' ' + ln[1:]
        else:
            st.append(ln)
    ports, caps, res, devn = [], [], [], set()
    for s in st:
        t = s.split(';')[0].split()
        if not t:
            continue
        k = t[0][0].upper()
        if t[0].lower() == '.subckt':
            ports = t[2:]
        elif k == 'C':
            caps.append((t[1], t[2], val(t[3])))
        elif k == 'R':
            res.append((t[1], t[2], val(t[3])))
        elif k == 'X':
            devn.update(x for x in t[1:-1] if '=' not in x)
    return ports, caps, res, devn


def base(n, names):
    """net a node belongs to: magic names the pieces of a resistive net <net>.n<k> / <net>.t<k>"""
    m = re.fullmatch(r'(.+)\.[nt]\d+', n)
    return m.group(1) if m and m.group(1) in names else n


def rmax(res, port):
    nodes = sorted({a for a, b, _ in res} | {b for a, b, _ in res})
    if port not in nodes or len(nodes) < 2:

        i, j = idx[a], idx[b]
        G[i, i] += g
        G[j, j] += g
        G[i, j] -= g
        G[j, i] -= g

cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_pex/_work/dbg && python3 - <<'EOF'
import re
from collections import defaultdict
S={'f':1e-15,'p':1e-12,'a':1e-18,'n':1e-9}
def v(s):
    m=re.fullmatch(r'([-+0-9.eE]+)([a-z]*)',s); return float(m.group(1))*S.get(m.group(2),1)
def load(f):
    L=[l.split() for l in open(f)]
    caps=[c for c in L if c and c[0][0]=='C']
    d=defaultdict(float)
    for c in caps:
        a,b=sorted(re.sub(r'\.[nt]\d+$','',x) for x in c[1:3]); d[(a,b)]+=v(c[3])
    return d
g=load('g.spice'); r=load('rh.spice')
pernet=lambda d: {n: sum(x for k,x in d.items() if n in k) for n in {y for k in d for y in k}}
pg,pr=pernet(g),pernet(r)
diff=sorted(((pr.get(n,0)-pg.get(n,0))*1e15,n,pg.get(n,0)*1e15,pr.get(n,0)*1e15) for n in set(pg)|set(pr))
for x in diff[-15:]: print('%.1f %s C=%.1f RC=%.1f'%x)
print(sum(x[0] for x in diff))
print([ (k,x*1e15) for k,x in r.items() if k[0]==k[1]][:10])
EOF
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Assemble the magic PEX netlists of extract.sh (magic 8.3.685, IHP sg13cmos5l tech files of the PDK).

Usage: python3 -I pexfix.py c  <hier.spice> <flat.spice> <out.spice>
       python3 -I pexfix.py rc <rc.spice> <c.spice> <res.ext> <out.spice> [<report.csv>]

c   Why: sak-pex.sh (the IIC-OSIC-TOOLS script that made the earlier pad PEX netlists) writes the netlist with
    'ext2spice lvs', i.e. hierarchical output. With hierarchical output, this magic writes the coupling capacitors
    but none of the node-to-substrate capacitances of the (flattened) top cell, although the .ext file has them
    (e.g. slot_7 net enable: 33.77 fF substrate capacitance in slot_7.ext, absent from the netlist; with
    'ext2spice hierarchy off' it is there as 'C enable vss_3v3 33.7651f'). The flat output has no .subckt line.
    This takes the .subckt line (port order) from the hierarchical netlist and devices + capacitors from the flat one
    (same devices; checked: the X cards are identical).
rc  Why: in the full-RC netlist (ext2spice extresist on) every resistive net N carries, at its resistor nodes
    ('rnode' lines of <cell>.res.ext), capacitors to the substrate net whose sum is N's TOTAL capacitance (coupling
    included), and the coupling capacitors of N are written as well, so the coupling of the resistive nets is counted
    twice, once of it as capacitance to ground (e.g. slot_7 dig_in[1]: substrate 3.66 fF, rnode capacitances 124.2 fF,
    total in the C extraction 127.9 fF). The full-RC netlist written here keeps devices and resistors of <rc.spice>
    and takes every capacitor of the C-coupled netlist <c.spice> (same node names for the nets' main nodes); a
    capacitor between a resistive net N and the ground net is split over N's resistor nodes in proportion to their
    rnode capacitance (the distribution magic computed), all other capacitors stay where the C netlist has them (at
    the nets' main nodes, as magic's own full-RC output does with the coupling capacitors).
    <report.csv>: per resistive net: rnode capacitance sum, C-netlist total and ground part, as evidence.
"""
import csv
import re
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

SUF = {'f': 1e-15, 'p': 1e-12, 'n': 1e-9, 'u': 1e-6, 'm': 1e-3, 'a': 1e-18}


def val(s):
    m = re.fullmatch(r'([-+0-9.eE]+)([a-zA-Z]*)', s)
    return float(m.group(1)) * (SUF.get(m.group(2)[:1].lower(), 1.0) if m.group(2) else 1.0)


def stmts(path):
    st = []
    for ln in open(path).read().splitlines():
        if ln.startswith('+') and st:
            st[-1] += ' ' + ln[1:]
        else:
            st.append(ln)
    return st


def header(path):
    for s in stmts(path):
        if s.lower().startswith('.subckt'):
            return s.split()
    raise SystemExit(f'{path}: no .subckt')


def cmd_c(hier, flat, out):
    h = header(hier)
    body = [s for s in stmts(flat) if s[:1] in 'XxCcRr']
    xh = sorted(s for s in stmts(hier) if s[:1] in 'Xx')
    xf = sorted(s for s in body if s[:1] in 'Xx')
    assert [re.sub(r'^\S+', '', s) for s in xh] == [re.sub(r'^\S+', '', s) for s in xf], 'device cards differ'
    nc = sum(1 for s in body if s[:1] in 'Cc')
    with open(out, 'w') as f:
        f.write(f'* magic C-coupled extraction, flat output (ext2spice hierarchy off) with the port order of the '
                f'hierarchical output; pexfix.py c\n* devices {len(xf)}, capacitors {nc}\n')
        f.write(' '.join(h) + '\n' + '\n'.join(body) + '\n.ends\n')
    print(f'{out}: {len(xf)} devices, {nc} capacitors (hierarchical output had '
          f'{sum(1 for s in stmts(hier) if s[:1] in "Cc")})')


def cmd_rc(rc, cnet, resext, out, report=None):
    gnd = None
    st_rc = stmts(rc)
    h = header(rc)
    # rnodes: name, capacitance (aF) per resistive net
    rn = defaultdict(dict)
    for ln in open(resext):
        t = ln.split()
        if t and t[0] == 'rnode':

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Magic parasitic extraction of the whole taped-out slot (final/gds/slot_7.gds, top cell slot_7) and of the two custom
# pad cells (pads/<cell>/<cell>.gds), and netgen LVS of each extraction (IHP sg13cmos5l; magic 8.3.685, netgen 1.5.324).
# Usage: extract.sh <work_dir>
# Per cell, in <work_dir>/<cell dir>/:
#   sak/        sak-pex.sh -m 2 exactly as the earlier pad PEX (../d2s_pex): C-coupled, flattened, 'ext2spice lvs'
#               -> <cell>.pex.spice. This output has NO node-to-substrate capacitance (see pexfix.py, README.md).
#   c/          the same magic commands (gds read, flatten, extract all, ext2spice lvs, cthresh 0.01 fF), written twice:
#               hierarchical (as sak-pex.sh, for the port order) and flat ('ext2spice hierarchy off', which writes the
#               substrate capacitances) -> pexfix.py c -> <cell>.pex.spice
#   rc/         full-RC: the commands of sak-pex.sh -m 3 with extresist threshold 0, minres 100 mOhm, mindelay 0
#               (resistance of every net magic finds resistance for, no delay gating) -> magic's netlist rc_magic.spice
#               -> pexfix.py rc (capacitors from c/, see there) -> <cell>.pex.spice
#   lvs_c, lvs_rc   lvs_collapse.py (capacitors dropped, parasitic resistors shorted) + netgen against
#               netlist/slot_7_netgen.spice (as netlist/netgen_check.sh) or pads/<cell>/<cell>.spice (vss port added
#               as ../d2s_pex/run.sh)
# Magic runs single-threaded, one at a time. Needs <work_dir>/design (fetch, for pex_add_port.py).
set -euo pipefail
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
SLOT=$(cd "$HERE/../../.." && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "$1")
export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks} PDK=${PDK:-ihp-sg13cmos5l}
export PDKPATH=$PDK_ROOT/$PDK
RCFILE=$PDKPATH/libs.tech/magic/$PDK.magicrc
SETUP=$PDKPATH/libs.tech/netgen/${PDK}_setup.tcl
S2D=sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff
D2S=sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single

head_tcl() {  # head_tcl <gds> <top cell> <subckt name> <ext dir>
  cat << EOF
crashbackups stop
drc off
gds read $1
load $2
select top cell
flatten ${2}_flat
load ${2}_flat
cellname delete $2
cellname rename ${2}_flat $3
select top cell
extract path $4
ext2spice lvs
EOF
}

extract_cell() {  # extract_cell <dir> <gds> <top> <subckt name>
  local d=$1 g=$2 top=$3 n=$4 t0
  rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p "$d/sak" "$d/c" "$d/rc"
  t0=$(date +%s)
  cp "$g" "$d/sak/"
  sak-pex.sh -d -m 2 -n "$n" -w "$d/sak" "$d/sak/$(basename "$g")" > "$d/sak/run.log" 2>&1
  { head_tcl "$g" "$top" "$n" "$d/c"
    echo "extract all"
    echo "ext2spice cthresh 0.01"
    echo "ext2spice -p $d/c -o $d/c/hier.spice"
    echo "ext2spice hierarchy off"
    echo "ext2spice -p $d/c -o $d/c/flat.spice"
    echo "quit -noprompt"; } > "$d/c/pex.tcl"
  magic -dnull -noconsole -rcfile "$RCFILE" "$d/c/pex.tcl" > "$d/c/magic.log" 2>&1
  python3 -I "$HERE/pexfix.py" c "$d/c/hier.spice" "$d/c/flat.spice" "$d/c/$top.pex.spice"
  { head_tcl "$g" "$top" "$n" "$d/rc"
    echo "extresist threshold 0"
    echo "extresist mindelay 0"
    echo "extresist minres 100"
    echo "extract do resistance"
    echo "extract do unique"
    echo "extract all"
    echo "ext2spice extresist on"
    echo "ext2spice cthresh 0.01"
    echo "ext2spice -p $d/rc -o $d/rc/rc_magic.spice"
    echo "quit -noprompt"; } > "$d/rc/pex.tcl"
  magic -dnull -noconsole -rcfile "$RCFILE" "$d/rc/pex.tcl" > "$d/rc/magic.log" 2>&1
  python3 -I "$HERE/pexfix.py" rc "$d/rc/rc_magic.spice" "$d/c/$top.pex.spice" "$d/rc/$n.res.ext" \
    "$d/rc/$top.pex.spice" "$d/rc/rnode_caps.csv"
  echo "  $top: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s; $(grep -E 'Nets extracted' "$d/rc/magic.log")"
}

lvs() {  # lvs <dir> <pex netlist> <top> <subckt name in pex> <reference> <netgen cell>
  local d=$1
  rm -rf "$d"; mkdir -p "$d"
  python3 -I "$HERE/lvs_collapse.py" "$2" "$d/lay.spice"
  if [ "$3" != slot_7 ]; then
    python3 -I "$WORK/design/scripts/pex_add_port.py" "$d/lay.spice

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
# Run the sim.py jobs of a job file one at a time (one ngspice process), skipping jobs whose log says ok already.
# Usage: runjobs.sh <work_dir> <job_file>
set -u
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "$1")
grep -v '^#' "$2" | while read -r j; do
  [ -z "$j" ] && continue
  python3 -I "$HERE/sim.py" "$WORK" "$j"
done
EOF
chmod +x runjobs.sh; cd _work && nohup ../runjobs.sh . ../jobs.txt > batch.log 2>&1 &
echo started

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Evaluate the sim.py runs (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, slot 7 with the slot extracted from final/gds/slot_7.gds).

Usage: python3 -I evaluate.py <work_dir> <dest_dir>
Reads <work_dir>/out/<view>_<mos>_<temp>_<vdd>_<analysis>[_<load>].<suffix>; writes <dest_dir>/csv/*_summary.csv,
<dest_dir>/csv/compare.csv, <dest_dir>/tables.md and <dest_dir>/plots/*.png.

Definitions (the same as the earlier folders, so the numbers compare):
  dc    (../dc_curves/rt.py) expected = v(vcm_s2d) + pad in - v(vref_s2d); err = pad out - expected; max |err| over
        pad in 0 ... 1 V; gain / offset = small-signal line from pad in = vref_s2d +- 0.1 V; 1 mV range = contiguous pad
        in range around vref_s2d with |pad out - small-signal line| < 1 mV
  ac    (../ac_tran/rt.py) gain at 1 kHz; f-3dB = first frequency above 1 kHz where the gain falls 3 dB below it;
        peaking = maximum above the 1 kHz gain; phase at f-3dB
  step  (../ac_tran/rt.py) settling to 1 % / 0.1 % of the step after the input edge start (2 us rise, 7 us fall),
        overshoot in % of the step, step gain = rise step / 0.5 V
  op    vcm = v(vcm_s2d), vref = v(vref_s2d) at pad in 0.5 V; I_slot = -i(Vslot), I_ring = -i(Vio) (enabled, and with
        enable 0 V: DC operating point, not the transient window of the designer's data sheet)
  pu    enable at 20 us: t_vcm98 / t_vref98 = last time v(vcm_s2d) / v(vref_s2d) cross 98 % of their value at 39.9 us,
        minus 20 us; t_out1mV = last time |pad out - its value at 39.9 us| > 1 mV, minus 20 us; currents at 39.9 us;
        peak I_ring during the supply ramp (0 ... 20 us) and after enable (20 ... 40 us)
  mode  max |pad out - expected| over pad in 0 ... 1 V (feed: the op), expected as the designer's run_modes.py
"""

    return float('nan')

import re
S={'p':1e-12,'n':1e-9,'u':1e-6,'m':1e-3,'f':1e-15}
def v(s):
    m=re.fullmatch(r'([-+0-9.eE]+)([a-z]?)',s); return float(m.group(1))*S.get(m.group(2),1)
for f in ['x_slot_7/c/slot_7.pex.spice','x_sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff/c/sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff.pex.spice','x_sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/c/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single.pex.spice']:
    n=0;bad=[]
    for l in open(f):
        t=l.split()
        if not t or not t[0].startswith('X') or 'mos' not in l: continue
        p=dict(x.split('=') for x in t if '=' in x); nodes=[x for x in t[1:] if '=' not in x]
        d,g,s,b=nodes[:4]
        for term,node in (('a',None),):
            pass
        if v(p['ad'])>1e-10 and d!=b: bad.append((t[0],'ad',p['ad'],d,b))
        if v(p['as'])>1e-10 and s!=b: bad.append((t[0],'as',p['as'],s,b))
        n+=1
    print(f,n,len(bad),bad[:4])
EOF

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Whole-slot magic PEX of the taped-out slot 7 (final/gds/slot_7.gds) and the two custom pads, netgen LVS, and the
# slot round-trip simulations with the extracted netlists (IHP sg13cmos5l; ngspice-47 with the IHP OSDI models,
# magic 8.3.685, netgen 1.5.324). Reproduces csv/, plots/ and tables.md of this folder.
# Usage: run.sh [<work_dir>] [fetch|extract|prep|stats|sim|eval|all]   (default ./_work, all)
#   fetch    design repository paths (../dc_curves/fetch.sh: commits 91a7da8b and 9052bd35, shallow, blobless)
#   extract  extract.sh: magic extraction (sak-pex.sh as before, C-coupled with substrate capacitance, full-RC) of
#            slot_7 and both pads, netgen LVS of each
#   prep     prep.py: simulation netlists (slot wrapper with the source port order, floating nodes tied)
#   stats    pexstats.py: parasitic totals -> csv/pex_totals.csv, csv/pex_nets.csv; rnode evidence -> csv/rnode_caps_*.csv
#   sim      runjobs.sh: the sim.py jobs of jobs.txt, one ngspice process at a time (about 4 h on 4 shared CPUs)
#   eval     evaluate.py -> csv/*_summary.csv, csv/compare.csv, tables.md, plots/*.png
# Needs: PDK_ROOT with ihp-sg13cmos5l, magic, netgen, ngspice with the OSDI models, sak-pex.sh (IIC-OSIC-TOOLS),
# python3 with numpy / matplotlib, git with network access to github.com.
set -euo pipefail
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "${1:-$HERE/_work}")
STEP=${2:-all}
export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks} PDK=ihp-sg13cmos5l
export PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1
mkdir -p "$WORK" "$HERE/csv" "$HERE/plots"

fetch() { "$HERE/../dc_curves/fetch.sh" "$WORK"; }
extract() { "$HERE/extract.sh" "$WORK" | tee "$WORK/extract.log"; }
prep() { python3 -I "$HERE/prep.py" "$WORK"; }
stats() {
  python3 -I "$HERE/pexstats.py" "$WORK" "$HERE"
  for c in slot_7 sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single; do
    cp "$WORK/x_$c/rc/rnode_caps.csv" "$HERE/csv/rnode_caps_$c.csv"
  done
}
sim() { "$HERE/runjobs.sh" "$WORK" "$HERE/jobs.txt" | tee -a "$WORK/batch.log"; }
evaluate() { python3 -I "$HERE/evaluate.py" "$WORK" "$HERE"; }

case "$STEP" in
  all) fetch; extract; prep; stats; sim; evaluate ;;
  fetch|extract|prep|stats|sim) $STEP ;;
  eval) evaluate ;;
  *) echo "unknown step $STEP"; exit 1 ;;
esac
```

<!--
SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
-->
# Slot 7: whole-slot parasitic extraction and simulation

**IHP sg13cmos5l post-layout (magic PEX) simulation** of the taped-out slot 7 as one extracted netlist, with the two
custom pads. Results and comparison: [results.md](results.md); generated table: [tables.md](tables.md).

## Question

Until now the slot-level simulations used the slot's source netlist (design repository commit 9052bd35) with
`pad_ref_rb` and `bias_dist_pd` KLayout-extracted, `bg_core_pd`, `s7_ctrl`, `ls_up_fs` and `s7_doutpd` from schematic
or source netlists, and the routing between the blocks not at all. The two pads were simulated as schematic, as magic
C-coupled PEX or KLayout-extracted. Nobody had extracted the whole slot, and nobody had extracted wire resistance.
This folder asks three questions:

1. Is the magic extraction of the whole `final/gds/slot_7.gds` the same circuit as `netlist/slot_7_netgen.spice`?
2. How large are its parasitics, with and without wire resistance?
3. How do the slot's DC, AC, step, power-up, `vcm` / `vref` and supply-current results change when the extracted slot
   replaces the source netlist, and when the slot and pads are full-RC?

## Method

1. **Extraction** ([extract.sh](extract.sh)), magic 8.3.685 with the PDK's magicrc. The slot is read from
   `final/gds/slot_7.gds` (sha256 `469f2327…`) and the pads from `pads/<cell>/<cell>.gds`. Each cell is flattened
   with the commands of `sak-pex.sh`. Three extractions are made per cell:
   - `sak`: `sak-pex.sh -m 2`, as the earlier pad PEX netlists (`../d2s_pex`, the S2D netlist of the design
     repository). It is kept for comparison only.
   - `c` (C-coupled): the same magic commands (`extract all`, `ext2spice lvs`, `cthresh 0.01` fF). The netlist is
     written flat (`ext2spice hierarchy off`) and given the port order of the hierarchical output
     ([pexfix.py](pexfix.py) `c`).
   - `rc` (full-RC): the commands of `sak-pex.sh -m 3`, with `extresist threshold 0`, `minres 100` mΩ and
     `mindelay 0`. With these settings magic extracts resistance on every net it finds resistance for: 169 of 388
     slot nets and 32 nets of each pad. The other nets have no resistor network in magic's output. The netlist keeps
     magic's devices and resistors and takes its capacitors from `c` ([pexfix.py](pexfix.py) `rc`, see below).
2. **Two corrections of the magic output, both found here:**
   - **Substrate capacitance.** Written hierarchically (`ext2spice lvs`, which `sak-pex.sh` uses), this magic
     version leaves out most of the node-to-substrate capacitance that the `.ext` file holds. Some nets also get a
     different value. The `.ext` totals are 2.162 pF for the slot, 1.893 pF for S2D and 1.999 pF for D2S. The flat
     output matches them: 2.158, 1.889 and 1.995 pF; the 4 fF difference is the 0.01 fF threshold. The hierarchical
     output gives 1.889, 1.447 and 1.339 pF. Examples: the slot net `enable` has 33.77 fF to substrate in the `.ext`
     and none in the hierarchical netlist; `s7_an_0_outp` has 3.47 fF in the `.ext` and 11.62 fF in the hierarchical
     netlist. **The pad PEX netlists of the earlier runs (`../d2s_pex`, `../dc_curves`, `../ac_tran`) are
     `sak-pex.sh` output, so they lack this capacitance:** 0.44 pF for S2D and 0.66 pF for D2S. The coupling
     capacitors are the same in both outputs. The devices are identical (checked card by card).
   - **Full-RC capacitance.** `extresist` places capacitors to ground on the resistor nodes of each resistive net,
     and their sum is the net's *total* capacitance, coupling included. `ext2spice` then writes the coupling
     capacitors as well. As a result magic's full-RC netlist has 3.90 pF for the slot instead of 2.16 pF. Example:
     `dig_in[1]` has 3.66 fF to substrate and 127.9 fF in all, and its rnode capacitances add up to 124.2 fF

```bash
cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_pex && kill 10586 10581; sleep 1; ps -eo pid,args | grep -E "runjobs|sim.py" | grep -v grep; python3 - <<'EOF'
pts=[('tt',27,3.3),('ss',-40,3.0),('ff',125,3.6),('fs',125,3.6),('sf',-40,3.0)]
L=['# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier','# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0',
 '# sim.py jobs, <view>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>:<analysis>[:<load>], priority order; run.sh runs them one at a time',
 "# PVT = the five points of the designer's tier-1 deck (run_tier1.py POINTS). Power-up (pu) runs take 20 ... 60 min",
 '# each on the extracted netlists (shared CPUs), so they come last and only at tt 27 C and ss -40 C']
def block(view,c,t,v,full):
    p=f'{view}:{c}:{t}:{v}'
    return [f'{p}:op', f'{p}:ac', f'{p}:dc']+[f'{p}:step:{l}' for l in (('open','10p','100p') if full else ('open','100p'))]
for view in ('c','prev','rc'):
    L+=block(view,'tt',27,3.3,True)
L+=['cc:tt:27:3.3:op','cc:tt:27:3.3:ac','cc:tt:27:3.3:dc','cc:tt:27:3.3:step:100p']
L+=['# solver check: c with SPARSE instead of KLU','cs:tt:27:3.3:op','cs:tt:27:3.3:ac']
L+=['# bias currents into the pads (iref lines), tt 27 C']+[f'{v}:tt:27:3.3:opi' for v in ('prev','c','cc','rc')]
for (c,t,v) in pts[1:]:
    for view in ('c','prev','rc'):
        L+=block(view,c,t,v,False)
L+=['# modes at tt 27 C (task 4)']
for m in ('swap','mon','feed','ext'):
    for view in ('c','prev','rc'):
        L.append(f'{view}:tt:27:3.3:mode_{m}')
L+=['# corrected pads at the other PVT points (AC and 100 pF step)']
for (c,t,v) in pts[1:]:
    L+=[f'cc:{c}:{t}:{v}:ac',f'cc:{c}:{t}:{v}:step:100p']
L+=['# power-up / enable']+['c:tt:27:3.3:pu','prev:tt:27:3.3:pu','rc:tt:27:3.3:pu','prev:ss:-40:3.0:pu','c:ss:-40:3.0:pu']
open('jobs.txt','w').write('\n'.join(L)+'\n')
EOF
grep -v '^#' jobs.txt | grep -vE '^c:tt:27:3.3:(op|ac|dc|step:open|step:10p|step:100p|pu)$' > _work/jobs_rest.txt; wc -l _work/jobs_rest.txt
cd _work && nohup sh -c 'while kill -0 11520 2>/dev/null; do sleep 5; done; ../runjobs.sh . jobs_rest.txt' >> batch.log 2>&1 &
echo ok
```

```bash
cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_pex && sed -i '/^c:tt:27:3.3:pu$/d; s/^# power-up from 0 V (supply ramp, uic): stalls on the extracted slot (c tt 27 C: at 2.778 us after 60 min); prev only$/# power-up from 0 V (supply ramp, uic): the extracted slot stalls (c tt 27 C: at 2.778 us after 60 min); prev only/' jobs.txt && cat > runjobs.sh.new <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
# Run the sim.py jobs of a job file one at a time (one ngspice process), skipping jobs whose log in <work_dir>/out
# already ends a complete run (SLOTDONE).
# Usage: runjobs.sh <work_dir> <job_file>
set -u
HERE=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
WORK=$(realpath -m "$1")
grep -v '^#' "$2" | while IFS= read -r j; do
  [ -z "$j" ] && continue
  IFS=: read -r view c t v ana load <<< "$j"
  tag="${view}_${c}_${t}_${v}_${ana}"
  [ "$ana" = step ] && tag="${tag}_${load:-open}"
  if grep -q SLOTDONE "$WORK/out/$tag.log" 2>/dev/null; then continue; fi
  python3 -I "$HERE/sim.py" "$WORK" "$j"
done
EOF
chmod +x runjobs.sh.new; ps -eo pid,ppid,args | grep -E "runjobs|sim.py" | grep -v grep
```

                                    'I_ring_peak_enable_uA', 'I_ring_before_enable_nA')]""")
s=s.replace("""            md_rows.append([q + (f' ({LN[ld]})' if ld else ''),""","""            md_rows.append([q.replace('39us', '19.9us' if ld == 'en' else '39us') + (f' ({LN[ld]})' if ld in LN else ''),""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

        dt = time.time() - t0
        log += (f'\\n=== attempt {k + 1}: {what or "as written"} ({line})\\n' + pr.stdout[-20000:] +

SRC_PORTS = {
    "sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff": "iovdd iovss vdd vss pad outp outn vcm vref iref en_3v3 en_3v3_b",
    "sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single": "iovdd iovss vdd vss pad vinp vinn vref iref en_3v3 en_3v3_b",
def wrapped(cell):
    RS.RUNS = RUNS
    flat = RUNS / RS.convert(cell)
    txt = flat.read_text()
    m = re.search(r"^\.SUBCKT\s+(\S+)\s+(.*)$", txt, re.M | re.I)
    lay_ports = m.group(2).split()
    txt = txt.replace(m.group(0), f".SUBCKT {cell}_lay {' '.join(lay_ports)}", 1)
    src = SRC_PORTS[cell].split()
    missing = [p for p in lay_ports if p not in src]
    assert not missing, (cell, missing)
    wrap = f"\n.subckt {cell} {' '.join(src)}\nXlay {' '.join(lay_ports)} {cell}_lay\n.ends {cell}\n"
    flat.write_text(txt + wrap)
    return flat.name


def main():

    txt = txt.replace(m.group(0), f'.subckt pad_ref_rb_lay {m.group(2)}', 1)
    src_ports = 'vddh vssh iref vcm vref en_3v3 en_3v3_b'
    assert sorted(src_ports.split()) == sorted(m.group(2).split()), m.group(2)
    out.write_text(SPDX + txt + f'\\n.subckt pad_ref_rb {src_ports}\\nXlay {m.group(2)} pad_ref_rb_lay\\n.ends pad_ref_rb\\n')
    print(out)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    NETS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    prb()''')
open(p,'w').write(s)
p='sim.py'; s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""        ps    as prev, with SPARSE""","""        hyb   as prev, with pad_ref_rb replaced by its magic C-coupled extraction (cut from slot_7.gds; prep.py)
        ps    as prev, with SPARSE""")
s=s.replace("""    if view in ('prev', 'ps'):""","""    if view in ('prev', 'ps', 'hyb'):""")
s=s.replace("""        other = [str(rdir / T.wrapped(c)) for c in ('pad_ref_rb', 'bias_dist_pd')]""","""        other = [str(rdir / T.wrapped(c)) for c in ('pad_ref_rb', 'bias_dist_pd')]
        if view == 'hyb':
            other[0] = str(NETS / 'pad_ref_rb_c.spice')""")
open(p+'.new','w').write(s)
EOF

<!--

# Slot 7 whole-slot PEX: results

**IHP sg13cmos5l post-layout (magic PEX) simulation.** Method, versions and limits: [README.md](README.md). Every
value in the comparison is in [tables.md](tables.md) and [csv/compare.csv](csv/compare.csv). Per-run values are in
`csv/*_summary.csv`.

## 1. LVS: the extraction is the taped-out circuit

netgen 1.5.324 compared each extraction, with capacitors dropped and parasitic resistors shorted
([lvs_summary.txt](lvs_summary.txt)):

| cell, extraction | layout devices (extracted → after merging) | reference | nets | result |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `slot_7`, C-coupled | 497 → 371 (`sg13_hv_nmos` 179, `sg13_hv_pmos` 268, `rhigh` 41, `pnpMPA` 9) | `netlist/slot_7_netgen.spice`: 443 → 371 | 222 = 222 | **Netlists match** (11 symmetries, resolved by property / name), 0 property errors |
| `slot_7`, full-RC | the same 497 → 371 (9 427 resistors shorted) | same | 222 = 222 | Netlists match, 0 property errors |
| S2D pad, C / full-RC | 225 → 76 | `pads/…/sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff.spice` | 37 = 37 | Circuits match uniquely; property errors only the schematic's `mm_ok` |
| D2S pad, C / full-RC | 293 → 76 | `pads/…/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single.spice` | 36 = 36 | Circuits match uniquely; property errors only `mm_ok` |

The slot's top level still reports "failed pin matching", for the same reasons as the hierarchical check of
[`signoff/taped_out_158afc5`](../../../signoff/taped_out_158afc5/README.md):

- The reference's comma ports (`analog_bus3,s7_an_0_vref`, `s7_an_0_en_3v3,s7_an_1_en_3v3`) are single nets in the
  layout, so the names cannot pair.
- `vss_1v2_uq0` has no partner.
- In the full-RC check, shorting the resistors also merges the port names that magic keeps as separate resistive
  pieces of one net (`s7_an_0_vcm`, `s7_an_0_vref`, `s7_an_1_vref`, `s7_an_1_en_3v3*`), so those ports appear as "no
  matching pin" as well.

The unused harness pins (`dig_in[6..23]`, `clk`, `reset`, `ibias0/1`, `vbias`) are disconnected in both netlists.

**Verdict:** the whole-slot extraction from `final/gds/slot_7.gds` is the circuit of `netlist/slot_7_netgen.spice`.

## 2. Parasitics

Totals from [csv/pex_totals.csv](csv/pex_totals.csv). Ground is the substrate node: `vss_3v3` in the slot, `iovss` in
the pads. "Floating" means nodes without a device or resistor: the `tughra_art` metal and poly, and unused metal.

| cell | extraction | capacitors | C total [pF] | to ground [pF] | coupling [pF] | floating nodes [pF] | resistors |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `slot_7` | `sak-pex.sh -m 2` (as the earlier pads) | 1568 | 1.889 | 0.760 | 1.128 | 0.160 (101 nodes) | – |
| `slot_7` | **C-coupled (with substrate C)** | 1636 | **2.158** | 1.029 | 1.128 | 0.160 | – |
| `slot_7` | **full-RC** | 3461 | 2.166 | 1.037 | 1.128 | 0.160 | **9 427** (169 of 388 nets) |
| S2D | `sak-pex.sh` (earlier pad PEX) | 573 | 1.447 | 0.562 | 0.886 | 0.019 | – |
| S2D | C-coupled / full-RC | 589 / 3436 | 1.889 / 1.889 | 1.003 | 0.886 | 0.019 | – / 12 048 |
| D2S | `sak-pex.sh` (earlier pad PEX) | 482 | 1.339 | 0.345 | 0.995 | 0.015 | – |
| D2S | C-coupled / full-RC | 503 / 3592 | 1.995 / 1.997 | 1.000 / 1.002 | 0.995 | 0.015 | – / 9 835 |

- The `.ext` files hold 2.162 pF (slot), 1.893 pF (S2D) and 1.999 pF (D2S). The flat output reproduces these totals
  to within the 0.01 fF threshold. **The hierarchical `sak-pex.sh` output, which all earlier pad PEX runs used, lacks
  0.27 pF in the slot, 0.44 pF in S2D and 0.66 pF in D2S.** All of that missing part is node-to-substrate
  capacitance: the bond pad's 116 fF (S2D) and 223 fF (D2S) are absent, and `enable` (33.8 fF) is absent. Some nets
  also get a value the `.ext` does not have: `s7_an_0_outp` has 11.6 fF instead of 3.5 fF.
- **Magic's own full-RC netlist double-counts capacitance.** It totals 3.90 pF for the slot, against 2.16 pF in the
  `.ext`. The resistor-node capacitances of a resistive
